The IPO Onslaught Is Forcing Bankers to Pick Teams

Both Goldman Sachs and Morgan Stanley are preparing for the dueling IPOs of OpenAI and Anthropic by forming separate teams to prevent information sharing between the AI giants.

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
What this means:
Goldman Sachs and Morgan Stanley are each forming walled-off internal teams to handle the competing OpenAI and Anthropic IPOs and prevent information leaking between the two AI giants.

内容:
Goldman Sachs 和 Morgan Stanley 正各自组建相互隔离的内部团队，分别操作 OpenAI 与 Anthropic 互相竞争的 IPO，以防两家 AI 巨头之间的信息泄露。

Why it matters:
Two of the largest private AI labs racing to public markets at once signals insiders are moving to monetize while valuations are peak. Dueling mega-IPOs are a textbook late-cycle supply event — the smart money cashing out is the classic top-of-bubble tell.

影响:
两家最大的私有 AI 实验室同时冲刺上市，说明内部人正赶在估值高点套现。同时出现的巨型 IPO 是教科书式的周期晚期供给事件——聪明钱离场往往是泡沫见顶的经典信号。

Revenue scaling without closing the loss gap is the core C3 tension: the business is growing the top line but burning more cash, and an IPO would shift that funding burden onto public markets. A richly-valued, loss-making OpenAI listing is exactly the kind of late-cycle liquidity event that marks the top of an AI-capex bubble.

影响:
营收扩张却没有收窄亏损，正是 C3 的核心矛盾：收入在涨，但烧钱更快，而 IPO 会把这笔融资负担转嫁给公开市场。一个高估值、持续亏损的 OpenAI 上市，恰恰是标志 AI capex 泡沫见顶的那种周期晚期流动性事件。

SpaceX Investor Powerlaw Debuts Amid IPO Race

Powerlaw, a closed-end fund that owns stakes in Elon Musk’s SpaceX and artificial intelligence firm OpenAI, traded well above the value of its underlying investments in its debut, capitalizing on retail investors’ clamor for exposure to the hottest private tech companies. Bloomberg's Anthony Hughes and Mike Dinsdale, CEO at Powerlaw join to discuss Powerlaw’s platform allowing exposure to private tech companies as well as the IPO enviroment.  (Source: Bloomberg)

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
What this means:
Powerlaw, a closed-end fund holding stakes in SpaceX and OpenAI, debuted trading well above the value of its underlying investments, driven by retail demand for exposure to hot private tech names.

内容:
Powerlaw 是一只持有 SpaceX 和 OpenAI 股权的封闭式基金，上市首日交易价格远高于其底层投资的实际价值，背后是散户对热门私营科技公司敞口的抢购需求。

Why it matters:

影响:

Why it matters:

Why it matters:

影响:
在万亿美元估值的讨论下，122% 的亏损率正是泡沫张力的核心：市场把 OpenAI 当作 AI 时代的压舱资产定价，但其单位经济模型深度为负。若这种叙事在 IPO 前发酵，将冲击支撑整个超大规模厂商资本开支的 AI capex 逻辑。

What this means:
A coalition of 42 US state attorneys general has opened a sweeping probe into OpenAI via subpoena, just days after its reported IPO filing. The subpoena targets ChatGPT's advertising, data practices, handling of minors, model sycophancy, and safety policies.

内容:

<a href="https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMihAFBVV95cUxQVWFSNTJEZlIzVjlyeXQ1RUZtZ2QxWTJ6RFJNSlMyT0ZWQmhyU25BeFY4bER5V0ZtYnBHLTlKbm1iaW80TXRFaFJ4RmdyYkUxZ2VzdVI5RDhpUkVJLUhUZnVleUs1U29raUFibkdJWktERUlhbFY2dGpReFBCRUFOVnJqajI?oc=5" target="_blank">Anthropic files for IPO before OpenAI as trillion-dollar startups race to go public</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<font color="#6f6f6f">AOL.com</font>

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
What this means:
A Google News RSS item (via AOL.com) reports that Anthropic has filed for an IPO ahead of OpenAI, framed as "trillion-dollar startups race to go public."

内容:
一则 Google News RSS 条目（来源 AOL.com）报道，Anthropic 已抢在 OpenAI 之前申请 IPO，标题称"万亿美元估值的初创公司争相上市"。

Why it matters:
Foundation-model labs rushing to public markets is a classic top-of-cycle tell — it pulls forward an exit and shifts AI-buildout funding from private capital onto retail/public investors, often right when private rounds get harder to raise. Watch whether the filing reveals real economics behind the AI-capex spend.

影响:
基础模型公司扎堆冲刺公开市场是典型的周期顶部信号——它把退出时点提前，并把 AI 建设的融资压力从私募资本转嫁到公开市场和散户身上，往往恰逢私募轮越来越难融的时候。关键要看招股书是否揭示出支撑 AI capex 的真实经济账。

42 states launch sweeping probe into OpenAI before IPO - msn.com

<a href="https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMipgFBVV95cUxPSjRSeVJXNjBqc3I1NXYxZEN1SEhVWVNWNEc3LVd4Sk85S3l3bEVkVVV6eUhnZkdJY0NHcU1vRUhnWHJ4MWNvMEJaa1h2M1NoaGNHeGNlUV9yaGd3QktCNmxhOHFnNWgtR1FxM0dPYnVHaEU2OWUxOHVYRHQ5bXlkVTZjWVp3Tk9POThRa1Vkam53UlZqd09PWk9UbXJTcTJoV0xPb1FB?oc=5" target="_blank">42 states launch sweeping probe into OpenAI before IPO</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<font color="#6f6f6f">msn.com</font>

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
What this means:
42 U.S. state attorneys general have opened a sweeping investigation into OpenAI ahead of its planned IPO.

内容:
42 个美国州的总检察长在 OpenAI 计划 IPO 前对其发起大规模联合调查。

Why it matters:
Regulatory and legal exposure at the sector's anchor company adds IPO-execution risk and could delay or reprice the offering that much of the AI-capex narrative is leaning on. A multi-state probe right before listing is the kind of headline that can crack sentiment around the whole AI build-out.

影响:
作为整个板块核心公司的 OpenAI 面临监管与法律风险，这会增加 IPO 执行的不确定性，可能推迟发行或压低估值——而当前大量 AI capex 叙事正押注于这次上市。临上市前的多州联合调查，正是那种可能动摇整个 AI 基建情绪的标志性事件。
